NEVERSINK RIVER AT NEVERSINK NY, USGS site 01436000 (USGS-01436000), stream in Sullivan County, New York, draining 92.6 square miles. Daily means from 1941-10-01 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
